_________ TestVectorizedFilter.test_dtype_batch_memory[False-1-uint8] __________

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad9687dc590>
dtype = <class 'numpy.uint8'>, batch_memory = 1, use_footprint = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E           AttributeError: module 'scipy.ndimage' has no attribute 'vectorized_filter'

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad968211d90>
dtype = <class 'numpy.int8'>, batch_memory = 1, use_footprint = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)

        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:

        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad9687b6290>
dtype = <class 'numpy.complex128'>, batch_memory = 48, use_footprint = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad9687b7410>
dtype = <class 'numpy.int16'>, batch_memory = inf, use_footprint = False

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3

            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)

        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`

        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]

_________ TestVectorizedFilter.test_dtype_batch_memory[True-48-uint8] __________

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad9680f56d0>
dtype = <class 'numpy.uint8'>, batch_memory = 48, use_footprint = True

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E           AttributeError: module 'scipy.ndimage' has no attribute 'vectorized_filter'

self = <scipy.ndimage.tests.test_filters.TestVectorizedFilter object at 0x2ad9680f5890>
dtype = <class 'numpy.int32'>, batch_memory = 48, use_footprint = True

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("dtype",
                             [np.uint8, np.uint16, np.uint32, np.uint64,
                              np.int8, np.int16, np.int32, np.int64,
                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)

                              np.float32, np.float64, np.complex64, np.complex128])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_memory", [1, 16*3, np.inf])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("use_footprint", [False, True])
    def test_dtype_batch_memory(self, dtype, batch_memory, use_footprint):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(435982456983456987356)
        w = 3
    
        if use_footprint:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, False, True])
            kwargs = dict(footprint=footprint, batch_memory=batch_memory)
        else:
            footprint = np.asarray([True, True, True])
            kwargs = dict(size=w, batch_memory=batch_memory)
    
        # The intent here is to exercise all the code paths involved in `batch_memory`
        # and `output` handling. To test the limited-memory case, `batch_memory=16*3`
        # is chosen to be just large enough for a *single* window of `complex128` to
        # fit, and `n` is large enough that a whole sliding window view of `uint8`s
        # *won't* fit.
        n = 16*3 + 1
        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)
    
        input2 = np.pad(input, [(1, 1)], mode='symmetric')
        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
>           res = ndimage.vectorized_filter(input, np.sum, **kwargs)
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E           AttributeError: module 'scipy.ndimage' has no attribute 'vectorized_filter'

        input = rng.integers(0, 42, size=(n,))
        input = input + input*1j if np.issubdtype(dtype, np.complexfloating) else input
        input = input.astype(dtype)

        ref = [np.sum(input2[i: i + w][footprint]) for i in range(n)]
        sum_dtype = np.sum(input2).dtype
    
        message = "`batch_memory` is insufficient for minimum chunk size."
        context = (pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message)
                   if batch_memory == 1 else contextlib.nullcontext())
        with context:
